+        {
+          key: "Turnaround time",
+          summary: "Lower is better",
+          baseline: 5,
+          pilot: 2,
+          unit: "days",
+          baselineLabel: "Baseline 5 days",
+          pilotLabel: "Pilot 2 days",
+          baselineScale: 1,
+          pilotScale: 0.4,
+        },
+        {
+          key: "Formatting rework",
+          summary: "Lower is better",
+          baseline: 14,
+          pilot: 4,
+          unit: "count",
+          baselineLabel: "Baseline 14",
+          pilotLabel: "Pilot 4",
+          baselineScale: 1,
+          pilotScale: 0.29,
+        },
+        {
+          key: "Reviewer satisfaction",
+          summary: "Higher is better",
+          baseline: 3.4,
+          pilot: 4.5,
+          unit: "score",
+          baselineLabel: "Baseline 3.4",
+          pilotLabel: "Pilot 4.5",
+          baselineScale: 0.76,
+          pilotScale: 1,
+        },
+        {
+          key: "Documents processed",
+          summary: "Higher is better",
+          baseline: 18,
+          pilot: 42,
+          unit: "count",
+          baselineLabel: "Baseline 18",
+          pilotLabel: "Pilot 42",
+          baselineScale: 0.43,
+          pilotScale: 1,
+        },
+      ];
+
+      const list = document.getElementById("bar-list");
+
+      function deltaText(metric) {
+        const diff = metric.pilot - metric.baseline;
+        const percent = metric.baseline === 0 ? 0 : Math.abs((diff / metric.baseline) * 100);
+        const rounded = Math.round(percent);
+        const direction = metric.key === "Turnaround time" || metric.key === "Formatting rework" ? "decrease" : "increase";
+        if (direction === "decrease") {
+          return `<span class="pill">-${rounded}%</span> improvement vs baseline`;
+        }
+        return `<span class="pill">+${rounded}%</span> improvement vs baseline`;
+      }
+
+      list.innerHTML = metrics
+        .map(
+          (metric, index) => `
+            <div class="bar-item appear delay-${Math.min(index + 2, 6)}">
+              <div class="bar-name">
+                <strong>${metric.key}</strong>
+                <span>${metric.summary}</span>
+              </div>
+              <div class="bar-track" aria-hidden="true">
+                <div class="bar-fill" style="--scale:${metric.baselineScale}; opacity:0.48;"></div>
+                <div class="bar-fill alt" style="--scale:${metric.pilotScale};"></div>
+              </div>
+              <div class="bar-values">
+                <span><small>B</small> ${metric.baseline}</span>
+                <span><small>P</small> ${metric.pilot}</span>
+              </div>
+            </div>
+          `,
+        )
+        .join("");
+
+      const bars = document.querySelectorAll(".bar-fill");
+      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
+        bars.forEach((bar) => {
+          bar.style.transform = `scaleX(${bar.style.getPropertyValue("--scale") || 0})`;
+        });
+      });
+    </script>
+  </body>
+</html>

+    <script>
+      const metrics = [
+        {
+          key: "Turnaround time",
+          summary: "Lower is better",
+          baseline: 5,
+          pilot: 2,
+          unit: "days",
+          baselineLabel: "Baseline 5 days",
+          pilotLabel: "Pilot 2 days",
+          baselineScale: 1,
+          pilotScale: 0.4,
+        },
+        {
+          key: "Formatting rework",
+          summary: "Lower is better",
+          baseline: 14,
+          pilot: 4,
+          unit: "count",
+          baselineLabel: "Baseline 14",
+          pilotLabel: "Pilot 4",
+          baselineScale: 1,
+          pilotScale: 0.29,
+        },
+        {
+          key: "Reviewer satisfaction",
+          summary: "Higher is better",
+          baseline: 3.4,
+          pilot: 4.5,
+          unit: "score",
+          baselineLabel: "Baseline 3.4",
+          pilotLabel: "Pilot 4.5",
+          baselineScale: 0.76,
+          pilotScale: 1,
+        },
+        {
+          key: "Documents processed",
+          summary: "Higher is better",
+          baseline: 18,
+          pilot: 42,
+          unit: "count",
+          baselineLabel: "Baseline 18",
+          pilotLabel: "Pilot 42",
+          baselineScale: 0.43,
+          pilotScale: 1,
+        },
+      ];
+
+      const list = document.getElementById("bar-list");
+
+      function deltaText(metric) {
+        const diff = metric.pilot - metric.baseline;
+        const percent = metric.baseline === 0 ? 0 : Math.abs((diff / metric.baseline) * 100);
+        const rounded = Math.round(percent);
+        const direction = metric.key === "Turnaround time" || metric.key === "Formatting rework" ? "decrease" : "increase";
+        if (direction === "decrease") {
+          return `<span class="pill">-${rounded}%</span> improvement vs baseline`;
+        }
+        return `<span class="pill">+${rounded}%</span> improvement vs baseline`;
+      }
+
+      list.innerHTML = metrics
+        .map(
+          (metric, index) => `
+            <div class="bar-item appear delay-${Math.min(index + 2, 6)}">
+              <div class="bar-name">
+                <strong>${metric.key}</strong>
+                <span>${metric.summary}</span>
+              </div>
+              <div class="bar-track" aria-hidden="true">
+                <div class="bar-fill" style="--scale:${metric.baselineScale}; opacity:0.48;"></div>
+                <div class="bar-fill alt" style="--scale:${metric.pilotScale};"></div>
+              </div>
+              <div class="bar-values">
+                <span><small>B</small> ${metric.baseline}</span>
+                <span><small>P</small> ${metric.pilot}</span>
+              </div>
+            </div>
+          `,
+        )
+        .join("");
+
+      const bars = document.querySelectorAll(".bar-fill");
+      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
+        bars.forEach((bar) => {
+          bar.style.transform = `scaleX(${bar.style.getPropertyValue("--scale") || 0})`;
+        });
+      });
+    </script>
+  </body>
+</html>

+    <script>
+      const metrics = [
+        {
+          key: "Turnaround time",
+          summary: "Lower is better",
+          baseline: 5,
+          pilot: 2,
+          unit: "days",
+          baselineLabel: "Baseline 5 days",
+          pilotLabel: "Pilot 2 days",
+          baselineScale: 1,
+          pilotScale: 0.4,
+        },
+        {
+          key: "Formatting rework",
+          summary: "Lower is better",
+          baseline: 14,
+          pilot: 4,
+          unit: "count",
+          baselineLabel: "Baseline 14",
+          pilotLabel: "Pilot 4",
+          baselineScale: 1,
+          pilotScale: 0.29,
+        },
+        {
+          key: "Reviewer satisfaction",
+          summary: "Higher is better",
+          baseline: 3.4,
+          pilot: 4.5,
+          unit: "score",
+          baselineLabel: "Baseline 3.4",
+          pilotLabel: "Pilot 4.5",
+          baselineScale: 0.76,
+          pilotScale: 1,
+        },
+        {
+          key: "Documents processed",
+          summary: "Higher is better",
+          baseline: 18,
+          pilot: 42,
+          unit: "count",
+          baselineLabel: "Baseline 18",
+          pilotLabel: "Pilot 42",
+          baselineScale: 0.43,
+          pilotScale: 1,
+        },
+      ];
+
+      const list = document.getElementById("bar-list");
+
+      function deltaText(metric) {
+        const diff = metric.pilot - metric.baseline;
+        const percent = metric.baseline === 0 ? 0 : Math.abs((diff / metric.baseline) * 100);
+        const rounded = Math.round(percent);
+        const direction = metric.key === "Turnaround time" || metric.key === "Formatting rework" ? "decrease" : "increase";
+        if (direction === "decrease") {
+          return `<span class="pill">-${rounded}%</span> improvement vs baseline`;
+        }
+        return `<span class="pill">+${rounded}%</span> improvement vs baseline`;
+      }
+
+      list.innerHTML = metrics
+        .map(
+          (metric, index) => `
+            <div class="bar-item appear delay-${Math.min(index + 2, 6)}">
+              <div class="bar-name">
+                <strong>${metric.key}</strong>
+                <span>${metric.summary}</span>
+              </div>
+              <div class="bar-track" aria-hidden="true">
+                <div class="bar-fill" style="--scale:${metric.baselineScale}; opacity:0.48;"></div>
+                <div class="bar-fill alt" style="--scale:${metric.pilotScale};"></div>
+              </div>
+              <div class="bar-values">
+                <span><small>B</small> ${metric.baseline}</span>
+                <span><small>P</small> ${metric.pilot}</span>
+              </div>
+            </div>
+          `,
+        )
+        .join("");
+
+      const bars = document.querySelectorAll(".bar-fill");
+      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
+        bars.forEach((bar) => {
+          bar.style.transform = `scaleX(${bar.style.getPropertyValue("--scale") || 0})`;
+        });
+      });
+    </script>
+  </body>
+</html>
